MA	<p>3cc6_d1e8</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>ChaI</i></span> is derived from Corynebacterium halofytica, and <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>FaeI</i></span> is derived from Flavobacterium aquatile N3. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p><strong>DNA Strand Question:</strong></p><p>Examine the DNA strand shown below.
